# ATLAS reading packet

Collected 2026-11-18T12:17:46+00:00. This file is context for the editor. It is not the edition.
Each item is a headline already fetched from a feed or a newsroom page. A text-reader item was taken from a page that blocks a normal download.
High confidence is still three independent sources, or five when the national-language and official mix is missing. Medium is two. Low is one, and the summary must say so.

- Tue, 17 Nov 2026 20:41:00 -0300 — Defesa pede que Supremo julgue pedido para soltar Vorcaro
  A defesa do ex-banqueiro Daniel Vorcaro protocolou nesta terça-feira (17) uma petição para solicitar que o Supremo Tribunal Federal (STF) julgue o pedido de soltura feito na semana passada. Em uma manifestação encaminhada ao presidente do STF, Edson Fachin, os advogados informaram que, de acordo com as regras processuais, a necessidade da manutenção da prisão deve ser renovada a cada 90 dias. - Tue, 17 Nov 2026 20:35:00 -0300 — STF inaugura símbolo da Justiça na tradição iorubá
  O Supremo Tribunal Federal (STF) inaugurou nesta terça-feira (17) a escultura do Oxê de Xango, símbolo da Justiça na tradição iorubá. A iniciativa é inédita no Supremo e faz parte das comemorações do Dia Nacional da Consciência Negra, celebrado na próxima sexta-feira (20). A incorporação da escultura ao acervo do Supremo foi solicitada ao presidente do Supremo, ministro Edson Fachin, pelo Instituto de Defesa dos Direitos das Religiões Afro-Brasileiras e outras entidades e líderes de religiões de matriz africana. - Tue, 17 Nov 2026 19:34:00 -0300 — Brasil avança a 2 semifinais do Mundial de tênis de mesa paralímpico
  O Brasil já tem garantidas, no mínimo, duas medalhas de bronze no Mundial de tênis de mesa paralímpico, em Pattaya (Tailândia), com a classificação dos paulistas Cátia Oliveira e Paulo Henrique Fonseca às semifinais. Eles voltam a competir nesta quarta (18) por um lugar mais alto no pódio. O país já soma um ouro nas duplas mistas , conquistado no último domingo (15). Cátia terá pela frente às 5h (horário de Brasília) a italiana Giada Rossi na semi da classe 2 (cadeirantes). Já Paulo Henrique duelará a partir das 8h com o norueguês Krizander Magnussen pela semi da classe 7 (andantes). Notícias relacionadas: Dupla brasileira é campeã mundial de tênis de mesa paralímpico. Thousands of defunct satellites and millions of debris fragments orbit Earth, increasing the risk of collisions. With the ClearSpace-1 mission (CS-1), Europe aims to demonstrate for the first time how a satellite can be captured and safely removed from orbit in a controlled manner. For this pioneering mission, the European Space Agency (ESA) has selected OHB System AG as prime contractor. By Matt McKinney It started with about 10 people, a carbon-fiber rocket that skeptics said would never work, and a sheep farm at the end of a windswept New Zealand peninsula. On Nov. 13, 2026, Electron flew for the 100 th time , reaching the mark faster than any other commercially developed rocket in history. Electron, which took roughly four years to develop, now rolls off the factory floor every eight days.   Astronomers have found a Long Period Radio Transient about 5,000 light years away that's different from other LPTs. Other LPTs have a white dwarf and a red dwarf, but this one has a white dwarf and a main sequence star. The exact cause of the binary pair's radio pulses isn't certain, but this work narrows it down. It also shows that these binaries could be common progenitors for Type Ia supernova explosions.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/this-rare-binary-star-emits-radio-pulses-every-few-minutes-will-it-eventually-explode
- Sun, 15 Nov 2026 13:22:00 +0000 — Navigating Lunar Orbit Using Nothing But Shadows And A Power Meter
  Humanity is quickly starting to saturate our ability to track all of the satellites we have in deep space. NASA’s Deep Space Network (DSN) has been the workhorse for tracking such things for decades, but is quickly being overwhelmed by the number of requests it receives. Which in turn makes it harder to track some of the missions that have been launched recently. A new paper, available in pre-print on arXiv, from Ian Down, James McElreath, and Manoranjan Majji, of Advanced Space, Space Exploration Engineering, and Texas A&amp;M respectively, offer a no-cost solution to that tracking problem - use solar array data from a spacecraft’s eclipse to determine where it is.
  https://www.universetoday.com/articles/navigating-lunar-orbit-using-nothing-but-shadows-and-a-power-meter

- Tue, 17 Nov 2026 19:34:00 -0300 — Brasil avança a 2 semifinais do Mundial de tênis de mesa paralímpico

- undated — TraCSS Registration for Spacecraft Operators
  TraCSS Registration for Spacecraft Operators &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content TraCSS Registration for Spacecraft Operators TraCSS encourages interested spacecraft operators to sign-up for the&nbsp; Traffic Coordination System for Space &nbsp;(TraCSS). Operators are encouraged to review the&nbsp; TraCSS User Agreement &amp; Data Policy &nbsp;prior to signing up. Note that at this&nbsp;time, TraCSS accounts are only available to organizations that own and/or operate spacecraft in orbit, and National Government users . Other entities (researchers, commercial&nbsp;entities, etc.) interested in receiving updates on the TraCSS program are encouraged to sign-up for the Office of Space Commerce “stakeholder engagement” contact lists,&nbsp; here . Sign-Up Process: As of May 2026, the Traffic Coordination System for Space is accepting satellite owner/operator and Nation
  https://space.commerce.gov/traffic-coordination-system-for-space-tracss/tracss-registration-for-spacecraft-operators/
- undated — Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification
  Dataset for Conjunction Assessment Verification &#8211; Office of Space Commerce &darr; Skip to Main Content Background: This test set was developed for the Traffic Coordination System for Space (TraCSS) and is designed to help space situational awareness (SSA) service providers test their conjunction assessment (CA) algorithms.   https://space.commerce.gov/dataset-for-conjunction-assessment-verification/

- undated — ULA Vulcan Anomaly Has Pentagon Implications
  ULA Vulcan Anomaly Has Pentagon Implications Skip to content One of the Pentagon’s two workhorses to orbit just got grounded—for the second time this year. ULA’s Vulcan rocket lifted off Sunday on its first mission for Amazon Leo, carrying 40 satellites onboard. However, an anomaly early in the flight resulted in the loss of all the satellites, triggering an FAA mishap investigation.&nbsp; “ULA exists to provide assured access to space for our nation and for the customers who depend on us,” the company wrote in a statement on X. “That purpose is why we take on some of the hardest missions in spaceflight, and why we hold ourselves to the standard of mission success. Today, we did not meet that standard.” The company announced Tuesday morning that the mission was lost 10 minutes into the flight, but that the problems started just after liftoff.   Space Cargo Unlimited Unveils REV1 Reentry Vehicle Skip to content Space Cargo Unlimited unveiled its new reusable reentry vehicle—called REV1—at Space Tech Expo in Bremen, Germany today.&nbsp; Developed in partnership with Thales Alenia Space, REV1 is designed to increase the total mass Space Cargo Unlimited can bring to orbit and back. The vehicle is targeting a first flight in 2030. “We have always believed that microgravity can become a new environment for industry, but that potential depends on building the right infrastructure.” Space Cargo Unlimited CEO Nicolas Gaume said in a statement.   Asynchronics Raises $3.1M To Boost its Digital-Twin Software Skip to content As a species, aerospace engineers typically give birth to twins: One spacecraft destined for orbit, and another digital replica to test before its sibling flies. As in real life: Twins are expensive. Polish engineering software startup Asynchronics today announced the closing of a $3.1M seed round to bring more affordable digital-twin software to the market, and grow its virtual catalog of off-the-shelf components to help space engineers more quickly build and test. b2venture led the round, which included additional participation from Movens Capital, HEARTFELT, and Hard2beat.   Senate Appropriators Release $1.072 Trillion Defense Bill &#8211; SpacePolicyOnline.com The Senate Appropriations Committee released its FY2027 defense spending bill last night. The total includes $1.072 trillion for DOD, essentially the same as the Trump Administration&#8217;s appropriations request, but it incorporates some of the additional spending the Administration wanted through a separate reconciliation bill. The U.S. Space Force would get $54.8 billion versus the $59.1 billion requested. President Trump is requesting a &#8220;generational&#8221; investment in defense, a total of $1.5 trillion for FY2027 of which $1.15 trillion is through the appropriations process and another $350 billion through a separate reconciliation bill.   Amid a more complex and unpredictable security environment, Canada is taking decisive actions to strengthen its security and sovereignty and to reinforce the resilience and long-term strength of the country. March 16, 2026 – Ottawa – National Defence / Canadian Armed Forces Amid a more complex and unpredictable security environment, Canada is taking decisive actions to strengthen its security and sovereignty and to reinforce the resilience and long-term strength of the country. Today in Ottawa, the Honourable David J. McGuinty, Minister of National Defence, announced an historic $200 million investment in core infrastructure for a Canadian-owned spaceport, along with other major new space-related capabilities and initiatives. This marks a significant step forward in Canada’s sovereign space program as outlined in [_Security, Sovereignty, Prosperity: Canada’s Defence Industrial Strategy_](https://www.canada.ca/en/department-national-defence/corporate/reports-publications/industrial-strategy.html). ### Canadian-owned spaceport Demand for timely and resilient launch options are at historic highs worldwide and sovereign access to space has become a critical capability underpinning nati

- undated — Vyoma to fly space-tracking sensors on 10 Kepler satellites
  Vyoma to fly space-tracking sensors on 10 Kepler satellites - SpaceQ Media Inc. Close Search for: Search Close Skip to content AETHER optical data relay network satellites testing. Credit: Kepler Communications Munich-based Vyoma said Tuesday it has signed an agreement with Kepler Communications to fly its space-tracking sensors on 10 of the Toronto company’s next-generation relay satellites, which are due to launch no earlier than October 2028. The sensors will ride on Kepler Communications’ Tranche 2 satellites, the next generation of an optical data relay network that moves data between spacecraft and down to the ground by laser. Kepler launched its first 10 relay satellites in January and started commercial service in August. Tranche 2 will go up over several launches in 2028: Vyoma’s group of 10 is on a dedicated rocket, and another 10 on a SpaceX Falcon 9 in mid-2028 that Maverick
  https://spaceq.ca/vyoma-kepler-space-tracking-sensors/

The company stood by its forecast of $41 million in revenue for 2026. On Tuesday, NorthStar said its shares will start trading on the Toronto Stock Exchange on Wednesday, under the same NSTR symbol it uses in New York. #### Rijksoverheid (Government of the Netherlands), space theme (government, nl, newsroom)

- undated — Defensie bouwt aan Space Command Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting ... 30-06-2026 17:00 Lees verder
  Defensie bouwt aan Space Command | Rijksoverheid.nl Nieuwsbericht 30-06-2026 | 17:00 Defensie zet de komende jaren een belangrijke stap in de verdere ontwikkeling van het militaire ruimtedomein. Met de oprichting van het Space Command bouwt Defensie verder aan het vijfde operationele domein (ruimte) naast land, zee, lucht en cyber. Dit is aangekondigd in de gisteren gepresenteerde Defensienota. Het doel is om in 2028 volledig operationeel te zijn met het Space Command . De basis voor militaire ruimteactiviteiten werd ruim 10 jaar geleden gelegd met kleinschalige experimenten. Inmiddels is het ruimtedomein uitgegroeid tot een operationeel onderdeel van Defensie, onder meer dankzij de inzet van eigen observatiesatellieten. Ruimte werd vorig jaar officieel ingericht als vijfde operationele domein. Daarom volgde de transitie van het Commando Luchtstrijdkrachten (CLSK) naar het Commando Lucht
  https://www.rijksoverheid.nl/actueel/nieuws/2026/06/30/defensie-bouwt-aan-space-command

- Tue, 17 Nov 2026 09:59:44 +0100 — Hera surveying asteroids with superhuman vision
  The European Space Agency’s Hera mission for planetary defence surveys its target asteroids with a combination of instruments, peering beyond the limits of the human eye. The larger Didymos asteroid – dominated by its dramatic equatorial ridge – is seen here from just 28.5 km away, variously in visible light, multispectral and thermal infrared vision.

Su misi&oacute;n principal es servir de nexo entre la tecnolog&iacute;a espacial y los usuarios, garantizando la explotaci&oacute;n de sistemas cr&iacute;ticos como Galileo y EGNOS, y fomentando el desarrollo de mercados y aplicaciones derivadas. EUSPA se erige como un pilar estrat&eacute;gico para la autonom&iacute;a y el liderazgo espacial europeo . #### MEDIA INAF (science, it, newsroom)

- undated — Un drone nel deserto per imparare ad allunare
  Un drone nel deserto per imparare ad allunare &#8211; MEDIA INAF Un drone sorvola un lago prosciugato nel deserto della California, eseguendo una serie di manovre che ricordano la discesa di un veicolo spaziale verso la superficie lunare. Non è una coincidenza: il volo fa parte di un esperimento della Nasa per rendere più sicuri e precisi i futuri allunaggi. Ma il drone sulla Luna non ci andrà mai: senza atmosfera, le sue eliche non potrebbero farlo volare. A interessare la Nasa è il sistema che trasporta, una tecnologia progettata per riconoscere i pericoli durante la discesa e aiutare i veicoli spaziali a scegliere autonomamente dove atterrare. Si chiama Splice , acronimo di Safe and Precise Landing &#8211; Integrated Capabilities Evolution , ed è un insieme di tecnologie di guida, navigazione e controllo sviluppate dall’agenzia spaziale statunitense per le future missioni sulla Luna,
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/11/10/un-drone-nel-deserto-per-imparare-ad-atterrare-sulla-luna/
- undated — Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna
  Come si tiene accesa una batteria sulla Luna &#8211; MEDIA INAF Chiunque abbia provato a usare uno smartphone lasciato per ore al freddo sa che batterie e basse temperature non vanno particolarmente d’accordo. Sulla Luna, però, il problema è decisamente più serio: al polo sud, durante la notte, le temperature possono scendere fino a circa 200 gradi sotto zero, mentre nelle zone illuminate dal Sole la superficie può superare i 100 gradi Celsius. E se in futuro astronauti, rover e strumenti scientifici dovranno trascorrere sempre più tempo da quelle parti, qualcosa dovrà pur tenerli accesi. È il problema al centro di SpearS, acronimo di Solid Power for Extreme Environment Adaptability and Resilience in Space , un progetto della Nasa appena partito sotto la guida di Donald Dornbusch , del Glenn Research Center di Cleveland, per sviluppare batterie allo stato solido capaci di resistere alle
  https://www.media.inaf.it/2026/10/19/come-si-tiene-accesa-una-batteria-sulla-luna/

#### StartMag (press, it, text-reader)

- undated — Cosa prevede il nuovo Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale
  Nel corso della seduta del Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), il governo Meloni ha approvato Documento strategico di politica spaziale nazionale. Tutti i dettagli Palazzo Chigi accelera su autonomia strategica e space economy. Ieri il governo Meloni ha approvato il nuovo Documento Strategico di Politica Spaziale Nazionale (Dspsn), predisposto dall’Agenzia Spaziale Italiana (Asi) in coordinamento con il Comint (Comitato Interministeriale per le politiche relative allo spazio e all’aerospazio), in attuazione degli Indirizzi del Governo in materia spaziale e aerospaziale. In Italia, il coordinamento politico dello spazio è gestito infatti dalla Presidenza del Consiglio dei ministri, con il Presidente del Consiglio che presiede il Comint. Il piano appena approvato definirà le priorità italiane nello spazio per il prossimo decennio. In particolare, il documento individua quattro assi strategici – ricerca e benefici per la società, competitività industriale, governance e cooperazione internazionale – e punta a rafforzare l’autonomia strategica del Paese, la sicurezza delle infrastrutture spaziali e la leadership industriale italian
  https://www.startmag.it/spazio-e-difesa/cosa-prevede-il-nuovo-documento-strategico-di-politica-spaziale-nazionale/

- undated — Was genau ist ein CubeSat? : Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders?
  Was genau ist ein CubeSat? - Austria in Space Was genau ist ein CubeSat? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Immer mehr Satelliten werden gelauncht, meistens sind es CubeSats. Doch wie sehen diese aus, und was macht sie so besonders? Früher waren Satelliten immer riesengroße, tonnenschwere Raumfahrzeuge, die ein hohes Budget, viel Zeit und viel Expertise verlangt haben. Nur wenige Nationen konntent sich solche Raumfahrt-Technologie leisten, was zur Folge hatte, dass es nur eine handvoll Weltraumnationen gab. Heute ist das anders, und das liegt an einem Würfel mit 10 cm Seitenlänge! Richtig, die Rede ist von einem CubeSat. Ein CubeSat, das ist ein kleiner Satellit, der aus einem oder mehreren würfelförmigen Modulen mit eben jeweils nur 10 cm Seitenlänge. Dabei sind diese Raumfahrzeuge nicht nur klein, sondern auch sehr leicht: Eine Einheit wiegt maximal 1,33 Kilo.
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/geschichte/was-sind-cubesats.php
- undated — Was versteht man unter Spacetech? : Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau?
  Was versteht man unter Spacetech? - Austria in Space Was versteht man unter Spacetech? Inhaltsverzeichnis einblenden Inhaltsverzeichnis ausblenden Upstream, Downstream, Satellitendaten – Der Weltraumsektor ist so viel mehr als Astronaut:innen im All. Doch was ist das alles genau? Jeder redet von Space Technology, aber was ist das überhaupt?&nbsp; Der Weltraumsektor ist im Wesentlichen zweigeteilt: In Upstream und in Downstream. Der Upstream Bereich umfasst die Beförderung von Objekten in den Weltraum, Bodenstationen und die Weltraumforschung, während als Downstream Daten und Services bezeichnet werden, die sich aus dem Upstream, vor allem aus Satelliten, ergeben.&nbsp;Eine Vielzahl an Anwendungen für die Erde fallen daher in den Downstream Bereich. Gerade darin liegt auch die Stärke des österreichischen Weltraumsektors. Doch auch europaweit liegt dort das größte Potential für die Wirtsch
  https://austria-in-space.at/de/artikel/was-ist-spacetech.php

#### BMIMI (Federal Ministry for Innovation, Mobility and Infrastructure) (government, de, newsroom)

- undated — Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen
  Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Weltraumaktivitäten der Vereinten Nationen Das Weltraumbüro (UNOOSA) und der Weltraumausschuss (UNCOPUOS) wurden 1958 von der Generalversammlung der Vereinten Nationen gegründet. Ziel des Ausschusses ist es, die friedliche internationale Zusammenarbeit im Weltraum zu fördern, die Erforschung des Weltraums zu unterstützen, sowie das Weltraumrecht weiterzuentwickeln. Österreich ist seit 1959 Mitglied des Weltraumausschusses. Seit 1993 befinden sich das Büro und der Ausschuss in Wien. Jährlich treffen sich Delegierte aus mittlerweile 100 Mitgliedsstaaten in Wien, um aktuelle und zukünftige Themen und Entwicklungen der Weltraumforschung und -nutzung zu diskutieren.&#xa0; Das Bundesministerium unterstützt die Arbeiten des Weltraumbüros insbesondere mit dem jährlich stattfindenden United Nations/Austria Symposium in Graz, sowie dem World Space Forum.
  https://www.bmimi.gv.at/themen/innovation/fti-themen/weltraum-und-luftfahrttechnologien/weltraum/weltraumbuero.html

  24 déc 2025 -16:21 > Appartient à Conseil des ministres du 23 décembre 2025 ## Programmation spatiale belge pour la période 2026-2030 Sur proposition de la ministre chargée de la Politique scientifique Vanessa Matz, le Conseil des ministres a pris acte des récents développements dans le secteur spatial international et des opportunités qui s'offrent aux entreprises spatiales belges dans ce même contexte. Il prend par ailleurs plusieurs décisions destinées à renforcer le secteur spatial belge. Les 26 et 27 novembre s’est tenue à Brême la réunion du conseil de l’Agence spatiale européenne (ESA) au niveau ministériel, qui couvrait tous les domaines d’activités pour la période 2026-2030. Au cours de cette réunion, les États membres étaient invités à adopter quatre résolutions et à s’inscrire aux différents programmes pour les cinq prochaines années. Dans ce contexte, le Conseil des ministres : approuve le cofinancement par la Défense de la participation belge aux programmes de l’ESA a dégagé 400 millions d’euros lors de l’élaboration du budget pluriannuel afin de permettre aux inscriptions belges d'atteindre un niveau satisfaisant et de d’octroyer à l'écosystème spatial belge suffisamm

- undated — tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie in 2025
  Nederland verhoogt inzet tijdens ESA-ministersconferentie in Bremen - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud Minister van Economische Zaken, Vincent Karremans, op ESA-ministerconferentie Nederland verhoogt de bijdrage aan de Europese ruimtevaartorganisatie ESA met 109 miljoen euro. Dit kondigde minister Karremans van Economische Zaken vandaag aan tijdens de ESA ministersconferentie in Bremen. De totale Nederlandse inzet tijdens de conferentie komt daarmee op 453 miljoen euro. NSO-directeur Harm van de Wetering reageert: “Hiermee zet Nederland een belangrijke stap in lijn met de politiek gesteunde Langetermijn-ruimtevaartagenda.” Eens in de drie jaar komen de voor ruimtevaart verantwoordelijke ministers van ESA-lidstaten bij elkaar tijdens de ESA-ministersconferentie. Daar besluiten ze over de toekomst van de Europese ruimtevaart. Dit jaar vond de conferentie plaats in Bremen. Op
  https://www.nlsa.nl/nederland-verhoogt-inzet-tijdens-esa-ministersconferentie-in-bremen/
- undated — 17 november 2026 Call open: 3,6 miljoen euro beschikbaar voor aardobservatie- en planeetonderzoek Lees meer
  Call open: 3,6 miljoen euro beschikbaar voor aardobservatie- en planeetonderzoek - Netherlands Space Agency Direct naar de inhoud De call ‘Gebruik van ruimte-infrastructuur voor aardobservatie- en planeetonderzoek (GO)’ is geopend. Met deze financieringsronde stimuleert NWO, in samenwerking met de Netherlands Space Agency (NLSA), wetenschappelijk onderzoek van hoge kwaliteit dat substantieel gebruikmaakt van infrastructuur in de ruimte. Voor de ronde is in totaal 3,6 miljoen euro beschikbaar. Nationale en internationale ruimtevaartorganisaties ontwikkelen en onderhouden satellieten en andere geavanceerde infrastructuur in de ruimte. Deze infrastructuur levert gegevens en signalen op die onderzoekers kunnen gebruiken voor wetenschappelijk onderzoek en maatschappelijke toepassingen. NWO wil samen met het ministerie van OCW en NLSA stimuleren dat Nederlandse onderzoekers deze mogelijkheden

  Vertrag unterzeichnet: VBS erhält Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich Vertrag mit Frankreich Amherd hat bald den Überblick Der Schweizer «Blindflug» ist bald zu Ende. Der Bund kann künftig Satellitenbilder aus Frankreich beziehen. Ein entsprechender Rahmenvertrag wurde unterzeichnet. Publiziert: 29.06.2023 um 11:12 Uhr | Aktualisiert: 29.06.2023 um 11:56 Uhr Teilen Kommentieren 1/5 Die Schweiz kann künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO beziehen (Symbolbild). Foto: AP Verteidigungsministerin Viola Amherd (61) macht einen weiteren Schritt auf das westliche Verteidigungsbündnis Nato zu. Soeben haben Frankreich und die Schweiz einen Rahmenvertrag unterzeichnet, der es dem Bund ermöglicht, künftig Satellitenbilder des französischen Aufklärungssatellitensystems CSO «Composante Spatiale Optique» zu beziehen. Selber hat die Schweiz keine Satelliten im All. Desha

Die Raumsonde Hera nahm den Himmelskörper aus einer Entfernung von nur 28,5 Kilometern auf. Vor rund zwei Wochen begann die Raumsonde Hera damit, den Doppelasteroiden Didymos/Dimorphos im Detail zu fotografieren, wie die Europäische Weltraumbehörde ESA in einer Pressemitteilung vom 17. November 2026 verkündete . Dabei kam nicht nur die Asteroid Framing Camera (AFC) zum Einsatz, sondern auch eine Reihe von Instrumenten zur Fernerkundung, die sowohl im Visuellen als auch im Infraroten arbeiten. Sand lasse sich womöglich als Windgeschwindigkeitsmesser ebenso für neu entstandene wie uralte Dünen im gesamten Sonnensystem nutzen.   --> HELLAS–SPACE 2.0: Προκηρύχθηκε ο πρώτος διαγωνισμός ύψους 69 εκατ. ευρώ – Η Ελλάδα αποκτά νέους οπτικούς δορυφόρους πολύ υψηλής ανάλυσης - ertnews.gr Μετάβαση σε περιεχόμενο Στην προκήρυξη του πρώτου διαγωνισμού του Εθνικού Διαστημικού Προγράμματος « HELLAS–SPACE 2.0 », προϋπολογισμού 69 εκατ. ευρώ, προχωρά το Υπουργείο Ψηφιακής Διακυβέρνησης και Τεχνητής Νοημοσύνης . Το έργο αφορά στην ανάπτυξη εθνικού αστερισμού τριών οπτικών δορυφόρων Παρατήρησης της Γης πολύ υψηλής χωρικής ανάλυσης, με στόχο την ενίσχυση της αυτονομίας της χώρας στην πρόσβαση σε δορυφορικά δεδομένα και την κάλυψη επιχειρησιακών αναγκών των Δημόσιων Υπηρεσιών. Ο διαγωνισμός διενεργείται από τον Ευρωπαϊκό Οργανισμό Διαστήματος (ESA), σε συνεργασία με το Υπουργείο Ψηφιακής Διακυβέρνησης και Τεχνητής Νοημοσύνης και το Ελληνικό Κέντρο Διαστήματος (ΕΛΚΕΔ). - undated — Αδριανός Γολέμης: Ο άνθρωπος που θα στείλει την Ελλάδα στο Διάστημα τους επόμενους 24 μήνες

NASA právě vybrala Axiom Space a Vast Space pro pilotované mise na ISS – příští léto může být průlomové. Za misemi nestojí jen astronauti, ale i šance pro český vesmírný průmysl a nové kontrakty.   https://www.e15.cz/byznys/podepsano-astronaut-svoboda-zna-prvni-termin-kdy-se-mu-otevre-okno-do-vesmiru-1431073
- undated — Česká cesta do vesmíru: stíhací pilot Svoboda by se tam měl podívat do pěti let
  Česká cesta do vesmíru: stíhací pilot Svoboda by se tam měl podívat do pěti let | e15.cz e15.cz Videopořady FLOW Česká cesta do vesmíru: stíhací pilot Svoboda by se tam měl podívat do pěti let FLOW: Rozhovor s astronautem Alešem Svobodou | Zdroj: e15 Veronika Jonášová 5. září 2024 · 05:30 Diskuze ( 0 ) Čtěte e15 v Googlu přednostně Nastavte si e15 jako preferovaný zdroj a naše články uvidíte ve vyhledávání výš. Preferujte nás Sledujte nás Česká republika má od roku 2022 poprvé od sedmdesátých let astronauta. Bojový pilot Aleš Svoboda uspěl v náročném výběrovém řízení Evropské kosmické agentury, kam se hlásilo víc než 22 tisíc lidí z Evropy. Svoboda je astronautem v záloze a teď mu svitla reálná naděje, že se do vesmíru skutečně podívá v rámci některé z misí Axiom. Jeho cestu podporuje vláda i premiér Petr Fiala. Politici jsou nyní nakloněni vesmírnou misi spolufinancovat. Vychází to přit

#### Defence24 (press, en, newsroom)

- undated — Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space
  Europe may be repeating its biggest mistake in space Menu It has become a truism that Europe’s defence industry and military systems are excessively fragmented, and therefore often more costly and less efficient than they would be if pooled at the European, or at least regional, level. Yet despite repeated warnings and countless recommendations, the continent now appears set to repeat the same mistake in space security, leaving itself particularly exposed at precisely the moment when space is becoming more strategically important than ever. European space capabilities are already deeply fragmented, especially in the defence sector. In contrast to continent-wide civilian programmes such as Galileo or Copernicus, military space assets remain scattered across the armed forces and governments of individual states. As a result, just four countries — France, Germany, Italy, and Spain — operate
  https://defence24.com/geopolitics/europe-may-be-repeating-its-biggest-mistake-in-space

  The Baltic as a ‘battlespace’: When diplomacy and Special Operations Forces work together [OPINION] Menu Following Russia’s full-scale invasion of Ukraine in 2022, Finland and Sweden decided to join the North Atlantic Alliance. It was at that point that the Baltic Sea began to be described, in simplified media shorthand, as a so-called »NATO inland sea«. For analysts and planners, however, it remains a maritime area riddled with gaps — in legislation, procedures and the architecture for sharing information. The recent ORKAN 26 exercise sought to identify those gaps before an adversary could exploit them.   Voyager 1 przekroczył granicę jednego dnia świetlnego Menu 18 listopada 2026 r., o godz. 11:16:07 czasu polskiego Voyager 1 stał się pierwszym obiektem stworzonym przez człowieka, który oddalił się od Ziemi na odległość jednego dnia świetlnego. Oznacza to, że sonda znalazła się 25,9 mld km od naszej planety, a więc w odległości, której pokonanie zajmuje światłu dokładnie 24 godziny. Przekroczenie tej symbolicznej granicy zajęło amerykańskiej sondzie niemal pół wieku, ponieważ została ona wystrzelona 5 września 1977 r.   Creotech Instruments rozpoczyna działalność w Niemczech Menu Creotech Instruments GmbH będzie w całości należeć do Creotech Instruments S.A. i pełnić funkcję ośrodka inżynieryjnego . Jej obecność ma wspierać współpracę z niemieckimi firmami i instytucjami oraz udział w europejskich projektach kosmicznych. Creotech Instruments ogłosił strategię rozwoju do 2029 roku „Chcemy, aby polskie technologie satelitarne rozwijały się w ścisłej współpracy z europejskim sektorem kosmicznym. - undated — Precyzyjne uderzenie dalekiego zasięgu z orbity? ATMOS i MBDA rozwiną koncepcję
  Precyzyjne uderzenie dalekiego zasięgu z orbity? ATMOS i MBDA rozwiną koncepcję Menu ATMOS Space Cargo GmbH (ATMOS) i MBDA Deutschland GmbH (MBDA) właśnie podpisały umowę o współpracy dotyczącą projektowania, rozwoju i demonstracji zdolności w zakresie precyzyjnego uderzenia dalekiego zasięgu z orbity (Precision Orbital Deep Strike; PODS) . Porozumienie łączy doświadczenie ATMOS w dziedzinie transportu orbitalnego i powrotu do atmosfery z kompetencjami MBDA w zakresie systemów obronnych i wojskowych ładunków użytecznych. Dzisiejsze oświadczenie potwierdza doniesienia z początku tego miesiąca, kiedy brytyjski dziennik „The Times” ujawnił to, co określił mianem „tajnej europejskiej misji budowy pocisków kosmicznych o nieograniczonym zasięgu” .   Človek je prvič poslal sondo na komet - Žurnal24 Prijava Facebook prijava 1/2 EPA Sonda Philea je uspešno pristala na površino kometa 67P/Čurjumov-Gerasimenko. Velik korak za civilizacijo, je podvig komentiral generalni direktor Evropske vesoljske postaje. Gre za prvi pristanek na kometu v zgodovini človeštva in zgodovinski dosežek Evropske vesoljske agencije (ESA). Do pristanka je prišlo nekaj minut po peti popoldne po našem času. Po ločitvi Philae in Rosette so znanstveniki v Nemčiji prejeli signal z Rosette, ki jim je začela pošiljati podatke, in tudi s Philae. Philae so tako lahko sledili vso pot do površja kometa. Zgodovinski poskus pristanka na kometu s premerom več kot štiri kilometre se je odvijal več kot 500 milijonov kilometrov stran od Zemlje.   Satellitter, infrastruktur og statlige romaktører Til hovedinnhold Satellitter, infrastruktur og statlige romaktører Norge har bygget opp en rominfrastruktur både til kommersiell og offentlig bruk. Norge har vært en romnasjon siden Andøya rakettskytefelt skjøt opp den første forskningsraketten i 1962. Siden har vi bygget opp rominfrastruktur tilpasset landets behov til en hver tid. Vårt medlemskap i ESA og samarbeid med EU har fått stor betydning for dette. Etter småsatellittenes inntog har Norge også bygget opp en flåte av egne nasjonale satellitter som skal dekke behov som vi ikke får dekket gjennom internasjonale samabeid. Nasjonale satellitter AISSat-1 (2010-2022) AISSat-1 var en pionér i Norge og i verden. Eier Direktoratet for romvirksomhet Hovedoppgave Skipsovervåking Dimensjoner 20x20x20 cm. 6 kg.   Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Til hovedinnhold Norge og internasjonalt samarbeid Bærebjelken i norsk satsing på romvirksomhet er deltakelse i internasjonalt samarbeid. I første rekke gjennom den europeiske romorganisasjonen ESA og EUs romprogram. I tillegg har Norge samarbeidsavtaler med enkeltland. Norge og ESA Den europeiske romorganisasjonen (ESA) er en internasjonal forsknings- og utviklingsorganisasjon grunnlagt i 1975. ESAs formål er å fremme samarbeid mellom europeiske stater innen romforskning og romteknologi for vitenskapelige formål og for utvikling av operasjonsdyktige romanvendelsessystemer. Samarbeid med ESA ESA er den Europeiske romorganisasjonen, og gjennom delegatvirksomheten administrerer Romsenteret Norges deltagelse i ESA. Photo: Wikimedia. Senators introduced bipartisan legislation this week aimed at streamlining the application process for new satellites. Senators Ted Cruz, R-Texas, and Peter Welch, D-Vermont, introduced the Satellite and Telecommunications Streamlining Act on January 14 which would set a one-year deadline for the FCC to make a decision on a license application. The stated goal of the legislation is to incentivize commercial satellite operators to base operations in the United States over foreign jurisdictions where satellite application processes may be less burdensome. Dr. Farhan: The Kuwait National Space Research Center is a national platform for excellence in space research. Kuwait University has signed an agreement with the Kuwait Foundation for the Advancement of Sciences (KFAS) to establish and operate the Kuwait National Space Research Center. The signing took place in the presence of Kuwait University President, Prof. Dina Musaed Al-Mailem, and KFAS Director General, Dr. Ameenah Rajab Farhan, alongside senior officials from both institutions. The center will operate under the auspices of KFAS, in collaboration with Kuwait University, with the aim of advancing scientific research in the fields of space science and technology, and supporting research and educational programs in
  https://vpaa.ku.edu.kw/media-center/news/kfas24

- undated — MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate
  MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate MIT Space Enabled estende afiliação do angolano Zolana João como Research Affiliate Sep 06, 2026 Destaques O Space Enabled Research Group, integrado no MIT Media Lab do Massachusetts Institute of Technology (MIT), estendeu a afiliação do angolano Dr. Zolana João como Research Affiliate (Investigador Afiliado), vínculo que mantém com o grupo desde 2023. A decisão dá continuidade a uma colaboração científica desenvolvida ao longo dos últimos anos com uma das instituições académicas de maior referência mundial nas áreas de ciência, tecnologia e engenharia. A extensão da afiliação reforça a presença de Angola em redes internacionais de investigação e inovação e reconhece a contribuição de investigadores angolanos para a produção de conhecimento sobre a utilização responsável, inclusiva e sustentável das tecno
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/mit-space-enabled-estende-afiliacao-do-angolano-zolana-joao-como-research-affiliate
- undated — Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space
  Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Lançamento Conecta Angola TV White Space Jul 23, 2026 Destaques Foi lançado, no dia 13 de Julho de 2026, o projecto Conecta Angola TVWS, uma iniciativa do MINTTICS, através do GGPEN e parceiros, que combina as capacidades do satélite ANGOSAT-2 com a tecnologia TV White Space (TVWS) para levar internet de banda larga a comunidades com acesso limitado à conectividade. A solução utiliza frequências não ocupadas do espectro televisivo para fornecer internet de forma eficiente, segura e com alcance de até 10 quilómetros, permitindo ampliar a cobertura digital e criar novas oportunidades de acesso a plataformas educativas, conteúdos digitais e serviços tecnológicos. O projecto foi lançado numa cerimónia realizada na Escola O Pensador, presidida pelo ministro das Telecomunicações, Tecnologias de Informação e Comunicação Social, Mário Oliveira, e contou c
  https://ggpen.gov.ao/en/news/lancamento-tv-white-space

- undated — Training – A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System
  NEREUS Training &#8211; A Space-Based Maritime Surveillance System &#8211; Mauritius Research and Innovation Council Skip to content Menu This training is the third and last of a series of courses within the Capacity Building programme for the NEREUS project funded by the United Nations Development Programme (UNDP) Ocean Innovation Challenge ( https://oceaninnovationchallenge.org ). The NEREUS project, led by the Surrey Space Centre (SSC) in collaboration with the Mauritius Research and Innovation Council (MRIC), aims at developing a tool, based on a Machine Learning algorithm to detect anomalies in maritime satellite data. Analysing these anomalies is very important in ensuring maritime security and environmental protection. The key application for this tool is the detection of potential Illegal, Unreported and Unregulated (IUU) fishing activities, to improve sustainable management of m
  https://spacemauritius.com/nereus-training-a-space-based-maritime-surveillance-system/

Overview Who we are The Zimbabwe National Geospatial and Space Agency (ZINGSA) is a wholly government-owned entity established in terms of the Research Act [Chapter 10:22] and officially launched by President Emmerson Mnangagwa in 2021. ZINGSA is responsible for promoting the peaceful use of space and applying geospatial and space technologies to support Zimbabwe&#8217;s socio-economic development and industrialization.   国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について : 宇宙政策 - 内閣府 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について 令和８年９月１日 内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局 １．概要 令和８年８月31日（月）、龍崎孝嗣内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局長は、「内閣府宇宙開発戦略推進事務局と米国連邦航空局（FAA）※との間の国際商業宇宙輸送に係る協力覚書」に署名を行いました。本協力覚書には、日本側から龍崎局長が署名を行い、米国側からベッドフォード連邦航空局長官が署名を行いました。 覚書締結に合わせて、龍崎局長から、本協力覚書を通じて、日米両国の法令や基準の調和を図り、日米共に更に安全で信頼性の高い商業宇宙輸送の拡大を目指す旨発言しました。ベッドフォード長官からも、引き続き連携を深めていく意向が示され、今後の協力の継続及び定期的な会合の実施を両者で確認しました。 ※米国連邦航空局（Federal Aviation Administration：FAA）は、米国における商業宇宙輸送活動の奨励、円滑化及び促進を図るため、商業宇宙輸送を規制する権限を有している。 ２．協力覚書の目的 本協力覚書は、商業宇宙輸送に適用されるベストプラクティスの共有及び産業標準の推進を行い、相互の関心事項及びプロジェクトに関して協力することを目的としています。 ３．対象とする協力活動概要 本協力覚書が推進する協力は、主に以下の項目です。 法令、規制、政策、プログラム及びプロジェクトに関する情報交換等 宇宙機の打上げ及び再突入、サブオービタル飛行、有人宇宙飛行並びに宇宙港に関する安全基準、リスク管理基準及び適合性評価における共通事項の検討 ベストプラクティスの共有 シンポジウム、ワークショップ、会議又はワーキンググループの共同開催 国際商業宇宙輸送に係る米連邦航空局（FAA）との協力覚書への署名について このページの先頭へ
  https://www8.cao.go.jp/space/kokusai/20260901/260901.html

#### ISRO (government, en, newsroom)

- undated — GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit.
  GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION Home / GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 has successfully placed NVS-03 in the intended orbit. October 28, 2026 GSLV-F18/NVS-03 MISSION GSLV-F18 is the 20 th flight of India’s Geosynchronous Satellite Launch Vehicle (GSLV). The GSLV-F18 vehicle placed NVS-03 satellite into a Geosynchronous Transfer Orbit.The Launch was successfully executed from the Second Launch Pad (SLP) at Satish Dhawan Space Centre, SHAR. NVS-03 is the Third Satellite in Next generation navigation satellite system envisaged for providing continuity of navigation services for IRNSS series. The satellite carries Indigenous Atomic clock, along with the procured Atomic clocks. It also includes L1 signal in addition to existing L5/S signals with Coverage over India land mass and a region extending 1500 km around Indian geopolitical boundary. GSLV-F18 on the launch pad More De

November 13, 2026 International Committee on Global Navigation Satellite Systems (ICG), established in 2005 under the umbrella of the United Nations is an important forum to promote voluntary cooperation on matters of mutual interest related to civil satellite-based positioning, navigation, timing, and value-added services. ISRO/DoS had the privilege to host the 20 th annual meeting of ICG (ICG-20) from October 25 to 30, 2026 along with the United Nations Office for Outer Space Affairs (UNOOSA) at Goa, India. Subsequent to the anomalies observed in the third stage of PSLV during the PSLV-C61/EOS-09 & PSLV-C62 / EOS-N1 missions, the configuration of the PS3 stage was revisited comprehensively w.r.t the design margins, raw material acceptance criteria, assembly procedures, and the quality checks that were followed so far. Thailand is tapping Nasa expertise to set up its northern “space lab” in Chiang Rai province. A Nasa delegation has been invited to visit the Space Technology Laboratory at Wiangpapao Technical College on January 25 to exchange knowledge on the development of space affairs. The knowledge exchange was agreed at a videoconference meeting between Nasa officials and the House committee on communications, telecoms and digital economy and society,its vice chair Settapong Malisuwan said on Tuesday. Thailand’s space agency will support the Royal Thai Armed Forces under a technology-sharing agreement announced on Monday. Under the agreement, the Geo-Informatics and Space Technology Development Agency (GISTDA) will share surveillance and other data to enhance national security and support missions by the military, GISTDA spokesman Siam Lawawirojwong said. The space agency will also boost skills among military personnel to protect Thailand&rsquo;s interests over land, sea, air and space, he added. Yutu-2, or Jade Rabbit-2, captured an obscure but intriguing image about 80 meters from its location during the mission's 36th lunar day.   VIENNA — A further extension of an enrichment facility in North Korea has been "externally complete" since last month, UN nuclear watchdog head Rafael Grossi said on Wednesday. Inspectors of the International Atomic Energy Agency (IAEA) have not had access to North Korea since 2009, with the agency relying on satellite imagery and open sources to monitor the country's nuclear programme. "Since mid-September 2026, a further extension to the original Yongbyon enrichment facility has been under construction with the new extension externally complete by late-October 2026," Grossi said in his opening statement at this week's Board of Governors meeting. - Wed, 18 Nov 2026 12:12:03 GMT — Korea, US discuss facilitating joint all-domain operations at high-level military meeting
  Top military officers of Korea and U.S. Forces Korea (USFK) on Wednesday discussed efforts to facilitate combined operations across all domains, with a focus on smooth intelligence sharing, the allies' Combined Forces Command (CFC) said. The South's Joint Chief of Staff Chairman Gen. Jin Yong-sung co-led the meeting with USFK Commander Gen. Xavier Brunson, who doubles as the CFC commander, for discussions on how the combined forces can work together to advance the strategy, known as combined joint all-domain operations (CJADO), the CFC said in a release. CJADO refers to a U.S. military concept to synchronize combat capabilities across land, air, sea, space and cyberspace into a single, networked force with allies like Korea. What began as a mission to explore the outer planets has become one of humanity’s longest-running scientific adventures, with the spacecraft now travelling through interstellar space. And for astrophysicist and University of Auckland Professor of Physics Jan Eldridge, that adventure helped inspire her own career. NASA's Voyager 1 spacecraft is depicted in this artist's concept traveling through interstellar space, or the space between stars, which it entered in 2012.
